[00:40:53.581–00:41:02.889] SPEAKER_44: The overarching regulatory framework that helps ensure that we are protecting the health of the community really starts at the Clean Air Act.

[00:41:03.249–00:41:09.415] SPEAKER_44: At its very simplistic state, the Clean Air Act sets ambient air quality standards for the entire country.

[00:41:10.816–00:41:21.525] SPEAKER_44: The rest of the Clean Air Act makes sure that if there are certain areas of the country that are not meeting those clean air standards, you have more stringent rules to ensure that you get back within those standards.
